### Węzeł `[180]`
Mówca: **Narrator**
> "You received a call from Vina. She had been trying to reach out to you for days.

Though Rizia and Pales were now unified, Duke Axel had managed to make a deal with the new government. Pales was able to maintain some degree of autonomy, and your family could take refuge there.
